# Gazette Notification for Publication

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 26 January 2024 No. 01 (E) dated 26 January 2024.—The President is pleased to award honorary commission to the rank of Honorary Captain/Lieutenant on the eve of Republic Day 2024 to the under mentioned Honorary Lieutenants/JCOs on active list under Para 177 of Regulation for the Army 1987, with effect from 26 January 2024 subject to the individual not being under any disciplinary action and satisfactory continued performance:TO BE HONORARY CAPTAIN (ON ACTIVE LIST) ARMOURED CORPS JC243107M RIS MAJ & HONY LT DIYAL SINGH JC244991H RIS MAJ & HONY LT NANAIAH M A JC244483M RIS MAJ & HONY LT RAVINDER SINGH JC245064F RIS MAJ & HONY LT SHAKTI SINGH RATHORE JC244805F RIS MAJ & HONY LT SHIV RAM SAINI JC244967M RIS MAJ & HONY LT SUSHIL KUMAR SINGH JC247860H RIS & HONY LT BHUPINDER SINGH JC247254M RIS & HONY LT JASBIR SINGH JC247304M RIS & HONY LT K RAMU JC246723X RIS & HONY LT RAM CHANDRA JC245561L RIS & HONY LT RICHHPAL JC247543F RIS & HONY LT SANJAYA KUMAR BEHERA REGIMENT OF ARTILLERY JC276506W SUB MAJ & HONY LT ABDUL RASEED JC274657W SUB MAJ & HONY LT ADIVEPPA MALAGI JC260122X SUB MAJ & HONY LT ARBIND KUMAR JHA RT JC259992H SUB MAJ & HONY LT ARUN KUMAR DIXIT RT JC276533A SUB MAJ & HONY LT BRAJABANDHU PANDA JC274256F SUB MAJ & HONY LT JAI PAL JC268316P SUB MAJ & HONY LT KUMBAR VEERBHADRA JC276959F SUB MAJ & HONY LT LOKHANDE VASANT GYANDEO JC273625F SUB MAJ & HONY LT MUKESH SINGH JC270512N SUB MAJ & HONY LT RAJ SINGH THEGAZETTEOFINDIA EXTRAORDINARY
